| es-capital-gains-0004 / LF23 | ## Who this applies to | [LIRPF art. 33](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a33) defines a gain/loss as a value change revealed by an alteration in asset composition, unless classified as another income category. | L art.33; L art.46 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a33; https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a46 | sources/L.reader.txt LF3160–3160: “variaciones en el valor del patrimonio del contribuyente que se pongan de manifiesto con ocasión de cualquier alteración en la composición de aquél, salvo que por esta Ley se califiquen como rendimientos.”; sources/L.reader.txt LF3160–3160: “alteración en la composición de aquél, salvo que por esta Ley se califiquen como rendimientos.”; sources/L.reader.txt LF3795–3795: “transmisiones de elementos patrimoniales.” | 2026-10-28; Express operative rule or stated bounded interpretation; actual precise source limits remain |
| es-capital-gains-0005 / LF23 | ## Who this applies to | A **transfer** gain normally enters the savings base | L art.33; L art.46 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a33; https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a46 | sources/L.reader.txt LF3160–3160: “variaciones en el valor del patrimonio del contribuyente que se pongan de manifiesto con ocasión de cualquier alteración en la composición de aquél, salvo que por esta Ley se califiquen como rendimientos.”; sources/L.reader.txt LF3160–3160: “alteración en la composición de aquél, salvo que por esta Ley se califiquen como rendimientos.”; sources/L.reader.txt LF3795–3795: “transmisiones de elementos patrimoniales.” | 2026-10-28; Express operative rule or stated bounded interpretation; actual precise source limits remain |
| es-capital-gains-0006 / LF23 | ## Who this applies to | a non-transfer gain enters the general base ([arts. 45–46](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a46)). | L art.33; L art.46 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a33; https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a46 | sources/L.reader.txt LF3160–3160: “variaciones en el valor del patrimonio del contribuyente que se pongan de manifiesto con ocasión de cualquier alteración en la composición de aquél, salvo que por esta Ley se califiquen como rendimientos.”; sources/L.reader.txt LF3160–3160: “alteración en la composición de aquél, salvo que por esta Ley se califiquen como rendimientos.”; sources/L.reader.txt LF3795–3795: “transmisiones de elementos patrimoniales.” | 2026-10-28; Express operative rule or stated bounded interpretation; actual precise source limits remain |

| es-capital-gains-0008 / LF25 | ## Who this applies to | An individual business's fixed-asset disposal can remain a capital gain under [art. 28.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a28) | L art.28; L art.33 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a28; https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a33 | sources/L.reader.txt LF2831–2831: “no se incluirán las ganancias o pérdidas patrimoniales derivadas de los elementos patrimoniales afectos a las mismas, que se cuantificarán conforme a lo previsto en la sección 4.ª de este capítulo.”; sources/L.reader.txt LF3186–3186: “transmisiones lucrativas por causa de muerte del contribuyente.”; sources/L.reader.txt LF3164–3164: “división de la cosa común.”; sources/L.reader.txt LF3220–3220: “transmisiones lucrativas por actos ínter vivos o a liberalidades.” | 2026-10-28; Express operative rule or stated bounded interpretation; actual precise source limits remain |

| es-capital-gains-0033 / LF49 | ## Rates, bands and thresholds | \| Over-65 insured-annuity reinvestment \| Six months; cumulative qualifying reinvestment ceiling 240,000 \| [LIRPF art. 38.3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a38) \| | R art.42 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a42 | sources/R.reader.txt LF2713–2713: “mayores de 65 años, siempre que el importe total obtenido por la transmisión se destine a constituir una renta vitalicia asegurada a su favor, en las condiciones que se establecen en este artículo.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2721–2721: “El contrato de renta vitalicia deberá suscribirse entre el contribuyente, que tendrá condición de beneficiario, y una entidad aseguradora.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2737–2737: “El incumplimiento de cualquiera de las condiciones establecidas en este artículo, o la anticipación, total o parcial, de los derechos económicos derivados de la renta vitalicia constituida, determinará el sometimiento a gravamen de la ganancia patrimonial correspondiente.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2715–2715: “seis meses desde la fecha de transmisión del elemento patrimonial.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2717–2717: “se destine íntegramente a constituir una renta vitalicia en el citado plazo de seis meses, el plazo para destinar el importe de la retención a la constitución de la renta vitalicia se ampliará hasta la finalización del ejercicio siguiente a aquel en el que se efectúe la transmisión.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2717–2717: “finalización del ejercicio siguiente a aquel en el que se efectúe la transmisión.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2725–2725: “periodicidad inferior o igual al año, comenzar a percibirse en el plazo de un año desde su constitución, y el importe anual de las rentas no podrá decrecer en más de un cinco por ciento respecto del año anterior.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2725–2725: “plazo de un año desde su constitución, y el importe anual de las rentas no podrá decrecer en más de un cinco por ciento respecto del año anterior.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2725–2725: “cinco por ciento respecto del año anterior.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2727–2727: “comunicar a la entidad aseguradora que la renta vitalicia que se contrata constituye la reinversión del importe obtenido por la transmisión de elementos patrimoniales, a efectos de la aplicación de la exención prevista en este artículo.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2729–2729: “240.000 euros.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2731–2731: “parte proporcional de la ganancia patrimonial obtenida que corresponda a la cantidad reinvertida.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2733–2733: “reinversiones anteriores, la cantidad de 240.000 euros, únicamente se considerará reinvertido el importe de la diferencia entre 240.000 euros y el importe de las reinversiones anteriores.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2735–2735: “intención de reinvertir en las condiciones y plazos señalados.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2739–2739: “autoliquidación complementaria, con inclusión de los intereses de demora, y se presentará en el plazo que medie entre la fecha en que se produzca el incumplimiento y la finalización del plazo reglamentario de declaración correspondiente al período impositivo en que se produzca dicho incumplimiento.” | 2026-10-28; | es-capital-gains-0053 / LF72 | ## The base / ### Ordinary listed and unlisted share disposals | For ordinary unlisted equity under article37.1(b), absent proof that the actual price is the amount independent parties would agree under normal market conditions, calculate the floor as the **greater** of (i) the net assets attributable to the shares in the last financial year closed **before tax accrual**, and (ii) the corresponding average results of the **three financial years closed before tax accrual, divided by20%**. | L art.37 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a37 | sources/L.reader.txt LF3383–3383: “El valor de transmisión así calculado se tendrá en cuenta para determinar el valor de adquisición de los valores o participaciones que corresponda al adquirente.”; sources/L.reader.txt LF3379–3379: “El valor del patrimonio neto que corresponda a los valores transmitidos resultante del balance correspondiente al último ejercicio cerrado con anterioridad a la fecha del devengo del Impuesto.”; sources/L.reader.txt LF3379–3379: “devengo del Impuesto.”; sources/L.reader.txt LF3381–3381: “capitalizar al tipo del 20 por ciento el promedio de los resultados de los tres ejercicios sociales cerrados con anterioridad a la fecha del devengo del Impuesto. | es-capital-gains-0093 / LF88 | ## The base / ### Main-home and annuity relief | 3. **Over-65 reinvestment of proceeds from any qualifying asset into an insured life annuity**, [LIRPF art. 38.3/RIRPF art. 42](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a42): For a positive total amount obtained on the disposal, set `eligible_reinvestment = min(total_amount_obtained, actual_qualifying_reinvestment, max(240,000 −prior_eligible_reinvestment, 0))` and calculate `exempt_gain = positive_gain ×eligible_reinvestment /total_amount_obtained`. | R art.42 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a42 | sources/R.reader.txt LF2713–2713: “mayores de 65 años, siempre que el importe total obtenido por la transmisión se destine a constituir una renta vitalicia asegurada a su favor, en las condiciones que se establecen en este artículo.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2721–2721: “El contrato de renta vitalicia deberá suscribirse entre el contribuyente, que tendrá condición de beneficiario, y una entidad aseguradora.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2737–2737: “El incumplimiento de cualquiera de las condiciones establecidas en este artículo, o la anticipación, total o parcial, de los derechos económicos derivados de la renta vitalicia constituida, determinará el sometimiento a gravamen de la ganancia patrimonial correspondiente.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2715–2715: “seis meses desde la fecha de transmisión del elemento patrimonial.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2717–2717: “se destine íntegramente a constituir una renta vitalicia en el citado plazo de seis meses, el plazo para destinar el importe de la retención a la constitución de la renta vitalicia se ampliará hasta la finalización del ejercicio siguiente a aquel en el que se efectúe la transmisión.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2717–2717: “finalización del ejercicio siguiente a aquel en el que se efectúe la transmisión.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2725–2725: “periodicidad inferior o igual al año, comenzar a percibirse en el plazo de un año desde su constitución, y el importe anual de las rentas no podrá decrecer en más de un cinco por ciento respecto del año anterior.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2725–2725: “plazo de un año desde su constitución, y el importe anual de las rentas no podrá decrecer en más de un cinco por ciento respecto del año anterior.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2725–2725: “cinco por ciento respecto del año anterior.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2727–2727: “comunicar a la entidad aseguradora que la renta vitalicia que se contrata constituye la reinversión del importe obtenido por la transmisión de elementos patrimoniales, a efectos de la aplicación de la exención prevista en este artículo.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2729–2729: “240.000 euros.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2731–2731: “parte proporcional de la ganancia patrimonial obtenida que corresponda a la cantidad reinvertida.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2733–2733: “reinversiones anteriores, la cantidad de 240.000 euros, únicamente se considerará reinvertido el importe de la diferencia entre 240.000 euros y el importe de las reinversiones anteriores.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2735–2735: “intención de reinvertir en las condiciones y plazos señalados.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2739–2739: “autoliquidación complementaria, con inclusión de los intereses de demora, y se presentará en el plazo que medie entre la fecha en que se produzca el incumplimiento y la finalización del plazo reglamentario de declaración correspondiente al período impositivo en que se produzca dicho incumplimiento.” | 2026-10-28; Express legal method plus labelled hypothetical separator/input and computed outputs, not invented legal figures |

| es-capital-gains-0118 / LF96 | ## The base / ### Dated main-home status extensions | For the article41bis.3 wording effective **8 October2026**, the two-year lookback is not the only route. | R art.41 bis | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a41bis | sources/R.reader.txt LF2673–2673: “por razón de su edad o situación de dependencia.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2673–2673: “trasladó su residencia a un centro especializado o al domicilio de un familiar hasta el tercer grado por consanguinidad o afinidad, por razón de su edad o situación de dependencia.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2673–2673: “o en el supuesto de transmisión por personas mayores de 65 años o por personas en situación de dependencia severa o de gran dependencia, hasta el momento en el que trasladó su residencia a un centro especializado o al domicilio de un familiar hasta el tercer grado por consanguinidad o afinidad, por razón de su”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2673–2673: “tercer grado por consanguinidad o afinidad, por razón de su edad o situación de dependencia.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2675–2675: “separación, divorcio o nulidad del matrimonio que hubieren determinado el cese de la ocupación efectiva como vivienda habitual para el cónyuge que ha de abandonar el domicilio habitual por tales causas, el requisito de ocupación efectiva de la vivienda habitual en el momento de la transmisión o en cualquier día de los dos años anteriores”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2675–2675: “cuando tal situación concurra en el cónyuge que permaneció en la misma.” | 2026-10-28; Express operative rule or stated bounded interpretation; actual precise source limits remain |
| es-capital-gains-0119 / LF96 | ## The base / ### Dated main-home status extensions | Where the transferor is over65 or has severe/great dependency, establish whether the property was their qualifying main home until they moved **because of age or dependency** to a specialised centre or the home of a relative within the **third degree of blood or affinity**. | R art.41 bis | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a41bis | sources/R.reader.txt LF2673–2673: “por razón de su edad o situación de dependencia.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2673–2673: “trasladó su residencia a un centro especializado o al domicilio de un familiar hasta el tercer grado por consanguinidad o afinidad, por razón de su edad o situación de dependencia.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2673–2673: “o en el supuesto de transmisión por personas mayores de 65 años o por personas en situación de dependencia severa o de gran dependencia, hasta el momento en el que trasladó su residencia a un centro especializado o al domicilio de un familiar hasta el tercer grado por consanguinidad o afinidad, por razón de su”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2673–2673: “tercer grado por consanguinidad o afinidad, por razón de su edad o situación de dependencia.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2675–2675: “separación, divorcio o nulidad del matrimonio que hubieren determinado el cese de la ocupación efectiva como vivienda habitual para el cónyuge que ha de abandonar el domicilio habitual por tales causas, el requisito de ocupación efectiva de la vivienda habitual en el momento de la transmisión o en cualquier día de los dos años anteriores”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2675–2675: “cuando tal situación concurra en el cónyuge que permaneció en la misma.” | 2026-10-28; Express operative rule or stated bounded interpretation; actual precise source limits remain |

| es-capital-gains-0172 / LF126 | ## The base / ### Urban property acquired in 2012 and non-resident old assets | For an individual resident, [LIRPF DA 37](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#datrigesimaseptima) exempts **50% of the gain** on a qualifying urban property **acquired for consideration from 12 May through 31 December 2012**. | L art.None; RDL18 art.None | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764; https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2012-6280 | sources/L.reader.txt LF10072–10072: “50 por ciento las ganancias patrimoniales que se pongan de manifiesto con ocasión de la transmisión de inmuebles urbanos adquiridos a título oneroso a partir de la entrada en vigor del Real Decreto-Ley 18/2012 y hasta el 31 de diciembre de 2012.”; sources/L.reader.txt LF10072–10072: “a partir de la entrada en vigor del Real Decreto-Ley 18/2012 y hasta el 31 de diciembre de 2012.”; sources/L.reader.txt LF10074–10074: “segundo grado incluido, a una entidad respecto de la que se produzca, con el contribuyente o con cualquiera de las personas anteriormente citadas, alguna de las circunstancias establecidas en el artículo 42 del Código de Comercio, con independencia de la residencia y de la obligación de formular cuentas anuales consolidadas.”; sources/L.reader.txt LF10076–10076: “una vez aplicada la exención prevista en esta disposición adicional, que corresponda a la cantidad reinvertida en los términos y condiciones previstos en dicho artículo.”; sources/RDL18.reader.txt LF486–486: “12/05/2012.”; sources/RDL18.reader.txt LF1396–1396: “entrará en vigor el mismo día de su publicación en el «Boletín Oficial del Estado».” | 2026-10-28; Express operative rule or stated bounded interpretation; actual precise source limits remain |

| es-capital-gains-0184 / LF132 | ## Deadlines and filing | Residents include the gain/loss and exemption/election evidence in **Modelo 100 for the accrual year**, using [LIRPF art. 14](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a14) timing and the approved campaign window. | L art.14; L art.96 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a14; https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a96 | sources/L.reader.txt LF1857–1857: “alteración patrimonial.”; sources/L.reader.txt LF7226–7226: “obligados a presentar y suscribir declaración por este Impuesto, con los límites y condiciones que reglamentariamente se establezcan.” | 2026-10-28; Express operative rule or stated bounded interpretation; actual precise source limits remain |

| es-capital-gains-0187 / LF134 | ## Deadlines and filing | If reinvestment conditions fail, [RIRPF arts. 41/42](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a41) require the specified original-year supplementary declaration with interest in the special interval ending with the filing period for the failure year. | R art.41; R art.42 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a41; https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a42 | sources/R.reader.txt LF2637–2637: “autoliquidación complementaria, con inclusión de los intereses de demora, y se presentará en el plazo que medie entre la fecha en que se produzca el incumplimiento y la finalización del plazo reglamentario de declaración correspondiente al período impositivo en que se produzca dicho incumplimiento.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2637–2637: “intereses de demora, y se presentará en el plazo que medie entre la fecha en que se produzca el incumplimiento y la finalización del plazo reglamentario de declaración correspondiente al período impositivo en que se produzca dicho incumplimiento.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2713–2713: “mayores de 65 años, siempre que el importe total obtenido por la transmisión se destine a constituir una renta vitalicia asegurada a su favor, en las condiciones que se establecen en este artículo.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2721–2721: “El contrato de renta vitalicia deberá suscribirse entre el contribuyente, que tendrá condición de beneficiario, y una entidad aseguradora.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2737–2737: “El incumplimiento de cualquiera de las condiciones establecidas en este artículo, o la anticipación, total o parcial, de los derechos económicos derivados de la renta vitalicia constituida, determinará el sometimiento a gravamen de la ganancia patrimonial correspondiente.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2715–2715: “seis meses desde la fecha de transmisión del elemento patrimonial.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2717–2717: “se destine íntegramente a constituir una renta vitalicia en el citado plazo de seis meses, el plazo para destinar el importe de la retención a la constitución de la renta vitalicia se ampliará hasta la finalización del ejercicio siguiente a aquel en el que se efectúe la transmisión.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2717–2717: “finalización del ejercicio siguiente a aquel en el que se efectúe la transmisión.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2725–2725: “periodicidad inferior o igual al año, comenzar a percibirse en el plazo de un año desde su constitución, y el importe anual de las rentas no podrá decrecer en más de un cinco por ciento respecto del año anterior.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2725–2725: “plazo de un año desde su constitución, y el importe anual de las rentas no podrá decrecer en más de un cinco por ciento respecto del año anterior.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2725–2725: “cinco por ciento respecto del año anterior.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2727–2727: “comunicar a la entidad aseguradora que la renta vitalicia que se contrata constituye la reinversión del importe obtenido por la transmisión de elementos patrimoniales, a efectos de la aplicación de la exención prevista en este artículo.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2729–2729: “240.000 euros.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2731–2731: “parte proporcional de la ganancia patrimonial obtenida que corresponda a la cantidad reinvertida.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2733–2733: “reinversiones anteriores, la cantidad de 240.000 euros, únicamente se considerará reinvertido el importe de la diferencia entre 240.000 euros y el importe de las reinversiones anteriores.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2735–2735: “intención de reinvertir en las condiciones y plazos señalados.”; sources/R.reader.txt LF2739–2739: “autoliquidación complementaria, con inclusión de los intereses de demora, y se presentará en el plazo que medie entre la fecha en que se produzca el incumplimiento y la finalización del plazo reglamentario de declaración correspondiente al período impositivo en que se produzca dicho incumplimiento.” | 2026-10-28; Express operative rule or stated bounded interpretation; actual precise source limits remain |

Repurchase deferral and capped loss offset are different ledgers | ```oa-check<br>name: Spain 2026 repurchase-blocked loss and savings-pool cross-offset<br>inputs:<br>  realised_transfer_loss: 10000<br>  deferred_repurchase_loss: 2000<br>  positive_investment_income: 10000<br>steps:<br>  admitted_loss: realised_transfer_loss - deferred_repurchase_loss<br>  cross_offset: min(admitted_loss, positive_investment_income * 0.25)<br>  admitted_loss_carryforward: admitted_loss - cross_offset<br>  savings_base: positive_investment_income - cross_offset<br>  savings_tax: min(savings_base, 6000) * 0.19 + max(savings_base - 6000, 0) * 0.21<br>expected:<br>  admitted_loss: 8000<br>  cross_offset: 2500<br>  admitted_loss_carryforward: 5500<br>  savings_base: 7500<br>  savings_tax: 1455<br>``` | L art.35; | actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF19 | Figures are for tax year 2026. The rates, bands, ceilings and coefficients below come from the consolidated texts of Ley 35/2006 (IRPF), Real Decreto 439/2007 (Reglamento IRPF) and Real Decreto Legislativo 5/2004 (IRNR) on boe.es, which carry the law in force. Two Agencia Tributaria pages are also linked. One of them sits in the "Manual práctico de Renta 2025", the return for 2025 that is filed during 2026, and it is used here only for the conditions of the over 65 main home exemption and for the filing window of that return, never for a rate. This Guide covers common territory Spain. The Basque provinces and Navarre run their own income tax and no page on an allowed host carries their capital gains rules. | Current supplied metadata/H1v1.4 and common-territory scope; old versions remain as history. |

Current Rates: state/autonomous66/76half-rates separately quoted and combined; minimum uses tax-on-minimum mechanism, not universalbase deduction. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF50 | The tax has a state half and an autonomous community half. For the GENERAL scale the community sets its own half (art. 74 Ley 35/2006), so there is no single Spanish general rate. For the SAVINGS scale the community sets nothing: art. 66.1 fixes the state half and art. 76 fixes the autonomous half at the same rates. That is why the savings scale is the same across common territory Spain. Art. 66.2, written for contributors who keep Spanish contributor status while habitually resident abroad, prints the two halves already added, which is the combined table above. | Current Rates: state/autonomous66/76half-rates separately quoted and combined; minimum uses tax-on-minimum mechanism, not universalbase deduction. |

| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF80 | **2. Selling the main home after 65 (art. 33.4.b Ley 35/2006).** The gain is not brought into the taxable base at all. No reinvestment is needed and there is no cash ceiling. The Agencia Tributaria states the conditions on https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c11-ganancias-perdidas-patrimoniales/ganancias-perdidas-patrimoniales-que-no-bi/ganancias-patrimoniales-exentas.html : the home transferred must have been the taxpayer's habitual residence for a continuous period of at least three years, and the taxpayer must have held the full ownership (pleno dominio) of it throughout that period, even if shared. Where full ownership is split between a bare owner and a usufructuary, neither of them gets the exemption. Art. 33.4.b itself states neither condition. The three continuous years come from art. 41 bis.1 Reglamento IRPF, which applies the main home definition expressly to art. 33.4.b. The pleno dominio point is the administration's reading: the Agencia Tributaria calls it an interpretative criterion set by the Supreme Court in judgment number 1858/2018 of 20 December. Treat it as settled practice rather than as words in the statute, and say so to a client who is a bare owner or a usufructuary. The relief applies whether the home is sold for a capital sum or for a temporary or life annuity, and to a sale of the bare ownership with the life usufruct kept back. It also applies to people in a situation of severe dependency or great dependency. | Current The base / Main-home and annuity relief plus dated status extensions: actualpositivegain/qualifyingproceeds/loan-net ratio, before/after2years, over65ownership and recapture conditions. Unrelated240000annuityceiling notimposedonhome. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF82 | A home still counts as the main home if it had that character on any day of the two years before the date of transfer (art. 41 bis.3 Reglamento IRPF). That is what lets someone who moved out shortly before selling still qualify. | Current The base / Main-home and annuity relief plus dated status extensions: actualpositivegain/qualifyingproceeds/loan-net ratio, before/after2years, over65ownership and recapture conditions. Unrelated240000annuityceiling notimposedonhome. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF84 | **3. Over 65 reinvesting ANY asset in a life annuity (art. 38.3 Ley 35/2006, art. 42 Reglamento IRPF).** This one is not about the home. A taxpayer over 65 who sells any asset can leave the gain out of charge by putting the whole amount obtained into an insured life annuity in their own favour within six months of the transfer. Unlike the two reliefs above, this one HAS a cash ceiling, and it is a lifetime total across all such reinvestments, not a yearly allowance. Once earlier reinvestments have used the ceiling up, only the difference counts as reinvested. Where less than the whole amount goes in, only the matching share of the gain is exempt. | Current The base / Main-home and annuity relief plus dated status extensions: actualpositivegain/qualifyingproceeds/loan-net ratio, before/after2years, over65ownership and recapture conditions. Unrelated240000annuityceiling notimposedonhome. |

| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF91 | Conditions the Reglamento adds for the annuity (art. 42): the contract is between the taxpayer, as beneficiary, and an insurance undertaking; the annuity is paid at intervals of a year or less; it must start being received within one year of being set up; the yearly amount may not fall by more than five per cent against the year before, which the Reglamento prints in words and not in digits; and the taxpayer must tell the insurer that the annuity is the reinvestment. Where the gain bore withholding and the transfer value less the withholding is put into the annuity within the six months, the deadline for the withheld part runs to the end of the following year. Taking the economic rights of the annuity early, in whole or in part, brings the gain back into charge. | Current The base / Main-home and annuity relief plus dated status extensions: actualpositivegain/qualifyingproceeds/loan-net ratio, before/after2years, over65ownership and recapture conditions. | actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF110 | How the ceiling bites, from letters c), d) and e) of the same rule: below it, the reduction is given in full; where this transfer takes the running total past it but the earlier total on its own is still below it, the reduction is given only on the slice of the transfer value that keeps the running total inside it; once the earlier total on its own is above it, no reduction at all. Guides routinely print the three coefficients and leave the ceiling out. Without the ceiling the answer is wrong for anyone who has sold more than one old asset since 1 January 2015. The ceiling counts TRANSFER VALUE, not gain. Shares and units in the investment vehicles the first two rows exclude are not left out of the rule: they fall into the third row, the residual 3.º of the same rule, which names no asset class and carves nothing out. | Current Pre-1994 abatement: completeDT9 days/special2005values/roundedholdingyears/classpercentages/cumulative400000transfer-cap; equality boundary explicit. |

That sentence sits inside letter c), so it does not survive letter e). | Current Pre-1994 abatement: completeDT9 days/special2005values/roundedholdingyears/classpercentages/cumulative400000transfer-cap; equality boundary explicit. |

| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF185 | Gains from immovable property situated in Ceuta or Melilla, and from movable property situated there, count as income obtained in those cities (art. 68.4.3.º letters d and e). Someone habitually and effectively resident in Ceuta or Melilla claims the credit on income obtained there. Someone who is not resident there claims it only on income obtained there, and only where the taxable bases are positive. Art. 68.4.2.º shuts income out of the credit for a taxpayer who is not resident in the cities: income from collective investment undertakings unless every asset is invested in Ceuta or Melilla, and the income in letters a), e) and i) of art. 68.4.3.º, which are employment income, gains on movable property situated there, and income from deposits and accounts with financial institutions there. So someone who does not live in the cities keeps the credit on a gain on immovable property situated there under letter d), but loses it on a gain on movable property situated there under letter e). | Current Loss pools/finaltax: conditional68.4 attributabletaxcredit, not a separatecapital-gainmarginaltable; specifiednonresidentmovablegain exclusions. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF187 | ## The method, step by step | Presentation heading/table separator, not a separate substantive tax rule. Current orderedordinarybasis/lot method, separateactualreliefs, loss pools and annualcombinedtax/prepayments, with twooaexamples. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF189 | 1. Settle residence and territory first. A Spanish resident is taxed on worldwide gains through Modelo 100; a non resident with no permanent establishment is taxed gain by gain through Modelo 210 under Real Decreto Legislativo 5/2004 (https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2004-4527). Common territory, the Basque provinces and Navarre are different tax systems, and this Guide is common territory only. | Current orderedordinarybasis/lot method, separateactualreliefs, loss pools and annualcombinedtax/prepayments, with twooaexamples. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF190 | 2. Decide whether there is an alteration at all. Art. 33.2 and 33.3 Ley 35/2006 treat some events as producing no gain or loss: dividing a common asset, dissolving the matrimonial property regime, separating co-owners, and transfers on the death of the taxpayer (https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764). | Current orderedordinarybasis/lot method, separateactualreliefs, loss pools and annualcombinedtax/prepayments, with twooaexamples. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF191 | 3. Put the item in the right base. A gain or loss on a TRANSMISSION is savings income (art. 46.b Ley 35/2006). A gain that is not on a transmission is general income (art. 45) and bears the general scale, which has an autonomous half this Guide does not carry (https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764). | Current orderedordinarybasis/lot method, separateactualreliefs, loss pools and annualcombinedtax/prepayments, with twooaexamples. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF192 | 4. Compute the gain. Use art. 35 for a sale, art. 36 for a gift or an inheritance, and art. 37 for listed securities, unlisted securities, collective investment funds and the other special cases. Where homogeneous securities exist, art. 37.2 treats the ones sold as the ones acquired first (https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764). | Current orderedordinarybasis/lot method, separateactualreliefs, loss pools and annualcombinedtax/prepayments, with twooaexamples. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF193 | 5. Apply any exemption before anything else: art. 33.4.b for the over 65 main home, art. 38.1 for reinvestment in a new main home, art. 38.2 for shares that carried the new company deduction, art. 38.3 for the over 65 life annuity. Check each one's own condition and its own ceiling in the sections above (https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764). | Current orderedordinarybasis/lot method, separateactualreliefs, loss pools and annualcombinedtax/prepayments, with twooaexamples. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF194 | 6. Apply the abatement coefficients only if the asset was acquired before 31 December 1994 and is not used in a business, and only after checking the cumulative ceiling in the table above (disposición transitoria novena, https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764). | Current orderedordinarybasis/lot method, separateactualreliefs, loss pools and annualcombinedtax/prepayments, with twooaexamples. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF195 | 7. Integrate and compensate. Net inside each pool, then cross between the two savings pools up to the capped share in the losses table, then carry any remainder to the four following years (art. 49, https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764). | Current orderedordinarybasis/lot method, separateactualreliefs, loss pools and annualcombinedtax/prepayments, with twooaexamples. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF196 | 8. Charge the savings scale in the table above (art. 66 Ley 35/2006), then apply the Ceuta and Melilla credit if it is in point (art. 68.4, https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764). Read the exclusions in art. 68.4.2 first: a taxpayer who does not live in the cities loses the credit on some of the income listed there, including a gain on movable property situated there. | Current orderedordinarybasis/lot method, separateactualreliefs, loss pools and annualcombinedtax/prepayments, with twooaexamples. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF197 | 9. File. A resident files Modelo 100 in the Renta campaign. The window for the 2025 return runs from 8 April to 30 June 2026, with bank direct debit to 25 June 2026; the Agencia Tributaria has not yet published the window for the 2026 return, which falls in 2027 (https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c01-campana-declaracion-renta/confirmacion-borrador-presentacion-declaraciones/plazo-forma-presentacion.html). A non resident selling property files Modelo 210 in the three months that begin one month after the transfer, and the buyer files Modelo 211 within one month of it (https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/no-residentes/irnr-sin-establecimiento-permanente/declaracion-irnr-sin-establecimiento-permanente/modelo-plazo-declaracion.html). | Current orderedordinarybasis/lot method, separateactualreliefs, loss pools and annualcombinedtax/prepayments, with twooaexamples. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF199 | ## Ask the client first | Current LF197, exact original unit |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF201 | - Were you a Spanish tax resident for the whole year, and in which autonomous community, or in Ceuta or Melilla? Residence decides Modelo 100 against Modelo 210, and Ceuta or Melilla brings in the credit, subject to the art. 68.4.2 exclusions for someone who does not live there. | Current six consequential intake questions. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF202 | - When did you acquire the asset? Anything acquired before 31 December 1994 opens the abatement coefficients, and the next question is which other old assets you have already sold since 1 January 2015, because the ceiling counts them all. | Current six consequential intake questions. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF203 | - Is the property your main home, and was it your residence for at least three years? Ask separately whether you held the full ownership, not a usufruct or the bare ownership on its own, and whether you were over 65 at the date of the transfer. | Current six consequential intake questions. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF204 | - If you are reinvesting in a new home, is the entire amount obtained going in, and was there a mortgage still outstanding on the old home? The outstanding principal comes off the amount you must reinvest. | Current six consequential intake questions. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF205 | - Have you bought the same shares, or homogeneous ones, in the two months either side of the sale, or the same asset within the following year? If so the loss is deferred, not denied. | Current six consequential intake questions. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF206 | - Do you have unused losses from the four previous years, and from which pool did they come? | Current six consequential intake questions. |

| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF1 | ---<br>name: es-capital-gains<br>description: Use this Guide to compute Spanish common-territory individual capital gains and losses for 2026. It covers transfer versus non-transfer classification, acquisition and disposal values, fees, improvements, depreciation, homogeneous-security identification, main-home reinvestment, over-65 relief, insured life annuities, pre-1994 abatement, repurchase-deferred losses, annual savings-pool integration and the current savings scale. Trigger phrases include capital gains Spain, ganancia patrimonial, sell shares, property gain, reinversión vivienda habitual, coeficientes de abatimiento, pérdida por recompra and compensación de pérdidas. Obtain the actual residence, asset and lot records, prices, eligible costs, prior-loss and repurchase history and relief evidence. The computation uses the taxpayer’s whole annual savings position and minimum allocation rather than taxing each sale through fresh first bands. Non-resident and special incoming-worker cases follow their companion routes.<br>version: 1.4<br>jurisdiction: ES<br>tax_year: 2026<br>last_updated: 2026-10-28<br>review_status: pending_review<br>trust_label: Built by Michael Cutajar and the OpenAccountants team<br>drafted_by: OpenAccountants<br>approved_by: pending<br>category: international<br>tier: 2<br>license: AGPL-3.0-or-later (code) / OpenAccountants Guide License v1.0 (content)<br>--- | Current LF1, exact original unit |

This revision is sourced from the law; independent whole-pack review is pending and no accountant has signed it off. | Current LF19, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF21 | ## Who this applies to | Current LF21, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF23 | [LIRPF art. 33](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a33) defines a gain/loss as a value change revealed by an alteration in asset composition, unless classified as another income category. A **transfer** gain normally enters the savings base; a non-transfer gain enters the general base ([arts. 45–46](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a46)). A long holding period does not itself create a lower tax rate. | Current LF23, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF25 | An individual business's fixed-asset disposal can remain a capital gain under [art. 28.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a28); distinguish it from selling trading stock. Do not label every business-related sale trading income. Death has the art. 33.3.b no-gain rule for the deceased; the recipient's inheritance tax is separate. A gift can produce a donor gain, while the donor's gift loss is not deductible. Partition/co-ownership events require the art. 33.2 conditions and do not permit an automatic basis uplift. | Current LF25, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF27 | This Guide covers common-territory IRPF. Use [es-income-tax](es-income-tax.md) for general/regional taxation and minimum allocation, [es-irpf-deductions](es-irpf-deductions.md) for regional credits, [spain-crypto-tax](spain-crypto-tax.md) for crypto-specific identification/reporting, [es-beckham](es-beckham.md) for an art. 93 election, and [the cross-border Guide](es-non-resident-income-tax-irnr-and.md) for ordinary IRNR and departure. Foral income tax, inheritance/gift tax, municipal urban-land tax and wealth tax require their own rules. | Current LF27, exact original unit |

| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF39 | The state/autonomous savings halves are respectively 9.5/10.5/11.5/13.5/15% in each slice, fixed by those articles. The ordinary general-income scale has a separately determined regional half. The savings tax is **not** computed by automatically subtracting the whole personal minimum from every gain: allocate available minima between general/savings and state/regional calculations under [arts. 56/66/76](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a56). Apply the tax-on-minimum mechanism where available, rather than simply tax `(gain −minimum)` through the progressive scale. | Current LF39, exact original unit |

| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF54 | ### Identify and value the transaction | Current LF54, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF56 | For an ordinary sale under [art. 35](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a35): | Current LF56, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF58 | `acquisition_value = acquisition_price + eligible_acquisition_costs/taxes + improvements −required_depreciation`. | Current LF58, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF60 | `transfer_value = actual_consideration_or_required_market_value −eligible_seller_transfer_costs/taxes`. | Current LF60, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF62 | `gain_or_loss = transfer_value −acquisition_value`. | Current LF62, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF64 | Exclude acquisition interest from basis. Distinguish capital improvements from repairs already deducted; do not deduct the same cost twice. For a let asset use the legally required depreciation adjustment, including the minimum depreciation, under [RIRPF art. 40](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a40). For gifts/inheritances use [LIRPF art. 36](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a36), including the succession-agreement and qualifying-business-gift carryover rules where applicable, not an invented cash purchase price. | Current LF64, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF66 | For listed shares, unlisted shares, funds, exchanges and other special transactions, [art. 37](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a37) overrides the ordinary method as stated. Use FIFO for homogeneous securities under art. 37.2. Unlisted-share minimum valuation and fund-transfer deferral have their own conditions; actual proceeds alone do not override the statutory test. Identify lots, acquisition dates and fees before calculating. | Current LF66, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF68 | ### Ordinary listed and unlisted share disposals | Current LF68, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF70 | Under article37.1(a), first establish whether the security belongs to the article's specified regulated-market population. For those listed equity disposals, use the **higher of the market quotation at disposal and the agreed price** as the statutory transfer reference, then apply the qualifying transfer-expense rules. Use the correctly established acquisition value, and FIFO for homogeneous securities under article37.2. A platform's description as listed is not itself the statutory market classification. | Current LF70, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF72 | For ordinary unlisted equity under article37.1(b), absent proof that the actual price is the amount independent parties would agree under normal market conditions, calculate the floor as the **greater** of (i) the net assets attributable to the shares in the last financial year closed **before tax accrual**, and (ii) the corresponding average results of the **three financial years closed before tax accrual, divided by20%**. The latter is capitalisation, not multiplication by20%. Include distributed dividends and allocations to reserves in that profit measure, excluding regularisation/revaluation reserves as the article specifies. Use at least that floor and the actual price if higher; verified independent-price proof permits the actual-price route. Use the statute's tax-accrual date, rather than silently substituting the disposal date. Apply qualifying seller expenses and the separately established acquisition value to calculate the gain. The statutory transfer value calculated under this paragraph also enters the buyer's acquisition-value determination. | Current LF72, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF74 | Example with otherwise ordinary, nonemployee shares, no special transaction or expenses and already established tax acquisition value EUR5,000: actual sale price EUR7,000, proportional net assets EUR10,000, and three-year average results attributable to the holding EUR3,000. Capitalised results are `3,000 /20% = EUR15,000`; the statutory floor is EUR15,000, so gain is **EUR10,000**, rather than EUR2,000 from cash proceeds alone. If valid independent-price proof establishes the EUR7,000 transaction price, the ordinary gain on the same assumed basis is EUR2,000. If actual price is EUR18,000 without that proof, use EUR18,000 and gain EUR13,000. | Current LF74, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF76 | For partially paid bonus shares, acquisition value is the amount actually paid. For fully paid bonus shares, spread the existing total cost across old and new shares, retaining the old shares' acquisition seniority for FIFO. Subscription-right disposal proceeds are a capital gain in the disposal year under the named article37 provisions; do not simply subtract them from the post-2016 share basis. | Current LF76, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF78 | For a2026 disposal of the listed securities covered by37.1(a), also apply [LIRPF DT29](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#dtvigesimanovena): deduct from acquisition value the proceeds of subscription-right sales **before1January2017**, excluding the part already taxed as a capital gain. If only some rights were sold, assign those rights to the securities acquired first. Keep the earlier rights/basis/tax history rather than applying only today's disposal-year rights rule to the entire holding. With original basis10,000, untaxed2016rights proceeds2,000 and otherwise ordinary2026sale15,000, adjusted basis8,000 gives gain7,000. If those2,000 rights proceeds were already taxed as a gain, do not deduct them again: basis10,000 gives gain5,000 on the same stipulated sale. | Current LF78, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF80 | Funds, tax-neutral exchanges and employee/share-acquisition reliefs keep their actual separate eligibility rules. The loss-repurchase restrictions and savings-netting method elsewhere in this Guide still apply. Source: [LIRPF article37.1(a)–(c)/2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a37). | Current LF80, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF82 | ### Main-home and annuity relief | Current LF82, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF84 | Keep these three reliefs separate: | Current LF84, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF86 | 1. **Main-home reinvestment**, [LIRPF art. 38.1/RIRPF art. 41](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a41): `exempt_gain = positive_gain ×min(qualifying_reinvestment /required_reinvestment_amount, 1)`. Required amount is the transfer value less outstanding principal on the qualifying acquisition loan. There is no general 240,000 ceiling for this home relief. Reinvest within two years before/after; state the reinvestment intention where it occurs later than the sale year. Check the new home's actual main-home entitlement. Qualifying rehabilitation needs the article's route; the structural-rebuilding alternative also requires cost **over 25%** of the applicable acquisition/market value excluding land. | Current LF86, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF87 | 2. **Main-home disposal by a person over 65 or with qualifying severe/great dependency**, [art. 33.4.b](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a33): no reinvestment requirement or monetary ceiling. Establish the actual main-home and ownership position. Under the [current AEAT ownership explanation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/vivienda-otros-inmuebles/que-ocurre-cuando-vendo-inmueble/transmision-vivienda-habitual-mayores-65-anos.html), a full owner over65 can qualify when transferring the bare ownership of the habitual home while reserving its life usufruct. This differs from a home whose full ownership was already divided between a bare owner and usufructuary: neither holder obtains this exemption on transferring that pre-existing right merely because the home was their habitual residence. Test the actual ownership immediately before the transfer and the other qualifying conditions; this ownership distinction does not replace the calculation of the gain on the transferred right. [RIRPF art. 41 bis](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a41bis) generally requires continuous three-year residence, twelve-month occupation after acquisition/completion, with statutory necessary-change exceptions, and permits the relevant main-home status in the two years before transfer. | Current LF87, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF88 | 3. **Over-65 reinvestment of proceeds from any qualifying asset into an insured life annuity**, [LIRPF art. 38.3/RIRPF art. 42](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a42): For a positive total amount obtained on the disposal, set `eligible_reinvestment = min(total_amount_obtained, actual_qualifying_reinvestment, max(240,000 −prior_eligible_reinvestment, 0))` and calculate `exempt_gain = positive_gain ×eligible_reinvestment /total_amount_obtained`. Record only this eligible amount against the cumulative allowance; unrelated annuity overfunding does not consume additional relief allowance. The exemption cannot exceed the positive gain. Use the disposal amount required by article42, not the gain itself or the home-reinvestment loan-net denominator. An annuity funded with60,000 against30,000 disposal proceeds and10,000 gain cannot exempt20,000: at most the10,000 gain is exempt on otherwise eligible facts; additional unrelated annuity funding does not create more exempt gain. With qualifying reinvestment15,000 against the same30,000 proceeds, the proportional exemption is5,000. The formula requires positive disposal proceeds; no zero-denominator or negative-gain exemption is asserted. Constitute the annuity within six months of disposal. Where the gain is subject to withholding, the extension for the withheld amount applies only if **all disposal proceeds net of that withholding** fund the annuity within those six months; reinvest the withheld part by the end of the year following disposal. For a 2026 disposal, that extended endpoint is 31 December 2027, not 1 January 2028. The taxpayer must notify the insurer that the contract reinvests the asset-disposal proceeds for this exemption. If qualifying reinvestment takes place after the disposal year, record the intention to reinvest in the disposal-year tax return. Neither notification replaces the actual investment or deadlines. | Current LF88, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF90 | The insured contract must name the taxpayer as beneficiary, pay at least annually, start paying within one year of constitution and reduce its annual payment by no more than 5% against the preceding year. For death-related features, [RIRPF DA 9](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#da) allows at most one potential reversion beneficiary; a guaranteed payment period cannot exceed ten years. Death-refund cover cannot exceed 95%, 90%, 85%, 80%, 75%, 70%, 65%, 60%, 55% and 50% of the amount invested in contract years 1–10 respectively, remaining at 50% from year 10 onward. [DT 18](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#dt) excludes contracts entered into before 1 April 2019 from DA 9. These safeguards apply where the corresponding feature exists; they do not require such a feature in every contract. | Current LF90, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF92 | Breach of an article 42 condition or full/partial anticipation of economic rights makes the corresponding gain taxable in the original gain year. File a supplementary return for that year with late-payment interest, during the period from the breach to the end of the ordinary filing period for the tax year of breach ([RIRPF art. 42.5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a42)). | Current LF92, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF94 | ### Dated main-home status extensions | Current LF94, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF96 | For the article41bis.3 wording effective **8 October2026**, the two-year lookback is not the only route. Where the transferor is over65 or has severe/great dependency, establish whether the property was their qualifying main home until they moved **because of age or dependency** to a specialised centre or the home of a relative within the **third degree of blood or affinity**. That specified former home retains the required status under this branch even where the ordinary two-year lookback has elapsed. It does not exempt a different investment property. Apply the underlying article33.4(b) or38 entitlement and all other applicable ownership/reinvestment conditions separately. | Current LF96, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF98 | If marital separation, divorce or annulment caused a spouse to leave the former main home, the actual-occupation condition at disposal or within the previous two years is deemed met for that departing spouse when it is met by the spouse who remained. Retain evidence of that marital situation and the remaining spouse's actual occupation. The cited clause does not itself state a court-order-only requirement; this method does not adjudicate the evidential boundary of an informal separation. | Current LF98, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF100 | For example, assume a73-year-old full owner sells on15October2026, with a positive gain EUR90,000. Their property was a qualifying main home until they moved in2022 to their granddaughter's home because of age, and all other article33.4(b) conditions are proved. The specified relocation branch satisfies main-home status despite the elapsed ordinary two-year window, so the EUR90,000 gain is exempt without reinvestment. This example does not claim the rule for an unrelated tenant's home, an unexplained voluntary move or an earlier disposal under a different operative wording. | Current LF100, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF102 | Use the dated legislative chain: [RDL29/2026 DF3.1 and DF11.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-20823) enact that wording from the day after7October publication. The same text was introduced by [RDL26/2026 DF3.1/DF11.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-20266), effective1October, followed by the [Congress repeal of2October](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-20526). Distinguish that1October interval, the2–7October interval and the wording effective8October; do not label the current extension retrospective for all2026 disposals. Any claim turning on the repeal's effect on an already accrued entitlement needs that exact dated analysis; the sources here do not adjudicate it. The general three-year residence, twelve-month occupation and justified necessary-change tests in [RIRPF41bis.1/2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a41bis) remain relevant. | Current LF102, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF104 | A property that is neither the taxpayer's main home nor eligible for the annuity relief is not exempt just because they buy another property. If entitlement or the required-reinvestment denominator is not proved, do not compute a full exemption from the new purchase price alone. | Current LF104, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF106 | ### Pre-1994 abatement | Current LF106, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF108 | Preserve the research trail, but apply the complete [DT 9](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#dtnovena) method: | Current LF108, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF110 | - Only qualifying assets acquired **before 31 December 1994** and outside economic-activity use qualify; de-affection must meet the provision's more-than-three-year rule. Separate improvements by their own acquisition/holding component. | Current LF110, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF111 | - Calculate the ordinary gain. For the general asset method, pre-20 January 2006 gain is `gain ×days_from_acquisition_through_19_Jan_2006_inclusive /total_holding_days`. | Current LF111, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF112 | - **Listed securities and relevant fund units have DT 9.1.2's special 2005 wealth-tax value method**, not that automatic linear-day split. If sale value is at least the prescribed 2005 value, take the eligible pre-2006 gain by substituting that value as sale value. If sale value is below it, all the positive gain is treated as pre-2006 under that rule. | Current LF112, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF113 | - Count pre-31 December 1996 holding years rounded up. The rate per year beyond two is 11.11% for the specified real estate/property-rich securities; 25% for qualifying listed shares; 14.28% for the residual assets. Apply the article's exclusions between those categories. The corresponding complete pre-2006 exclusion uses holding periods over ten/five/eight years respectively. | Current LF113, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF114 | - Check cumulative qualifying **transfer values**, not gains, since 1 January 2015. For a disposal crossing the 400,000 ceiling, apply abatement only to the pre-2006-gain fraction corresponding to the unused transfer-value capacity. Prior use above the cap leaves no current capacity. Keep the actual prior-disposal ledger; do not assume 400,000 per asset or per year. The exact boundary wording and an exceptional fact pattern need verification where it changes the claim. | Current LF114, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF116 | ### Loss pools and final tax | Current LF116, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF118 | Under [art. 49](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a49), first net investment-income items together, and independently net admitted transfer gains/losses together. If one pool is negative and the other positive, cross-offset only up to **25% of the positive pool**. Carry admitted unused losses for four following years in the statutory order and maximum permitted amounts; preserve origin-year/type balances and do not restart their clock. General non-transfer gains/losses use [art. 48](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a48), not the savings pool. | Current LF118, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF120 | Before pooling, defer repurchase-blocked losses under art. 33.5 until the corresponding retained asset/securities are later disposed of. A deferred loss is not the same as a currently admitted loss carried forward for four years. Other art. 33.5 losses from consumption, unproved losses, gifts and gambling excess are not automatically deductible. | Current LF120, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF122 | Integrate with all other savings income, apply available minima as above, then the combined savings scale and actual credits/payments on account. Compute **incremental gain tax** as annual tax with the relevant taxable item less annual tax without it where other income changes the bands; do not apply the first 6,000 band again to each disposal. Ceuta/Melilla uses the conditional art. 68.4 credit against attributable tax, not a different capital-gain marginal table; the non-resident-in-those-cities exclusions matter for movable-property gains ([art. 68.4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a68)). | Current LF122, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF124 | ### Urban property acquired in 2012 and non-resident old assets | Current LF124, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF126 | For an individual resident, [LIRPF DA 37](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#datrigesimaseptima) exempts **50% of the gain** on a qualifying urban property **acquired for consideration from 12 May through 31 December 2012**. Exclude acquisition from or transfer to the spouse, relatives by blood or affinity through the second degree, and the specified art. 42 group-connected entity, applying the statutory family/entity connection test. Do not halve a loss, proceeds or every property bought before 2013. For an eligible main-home reinvestment, apply this exemption **first**, then the proportional reinvestment exemption to the remaining gain under the incorporated conditions. | Current LF126, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF128 | A non-resident **without a Spanish PE** has the separate [TRLIRNR DA 4](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2004-4527#dacuarta)50% urban-property gain exemption for the same acquisition window, with the incorporated individual family/entity exclusions and the special entity-related exclusions where the transferor is an entity. Establish acquisition documents, urban classification, parties and connection before applying it. Purchaser withholding remains 3% of agreed consideration, credited against final tax; it does not become 1.5% because half the gain is exempt. [TRLIRNR DT 1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2004-4527#dt) preserves the specified old-asset transitions for non-PE individuals. A pre-1994 non-resident acquisition is therefore not automatically outside abatement: use the actual incorporated transition, eligible pre-2006 gain, asset class, residence and cumulative-disposal history. [AEAT’s current non-resident pre-1994 guidance](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/no-residentes/irnr-sin-establecimiento-permanente/determinacion-bi-irnr-sin-establecimiento-permanente/ganancias-patrimon-proced-bienes-adquiridos-1994.html), read 2026-10-27, expressly applies the 400,000 cumulative qualifying transfer-value cap for gains accrued from 1 January 2015. It also supplies the pre 20 January 2006 allocation,11.11%/25%/14.28% asset-class abatements for qualifying years to 31 December 1996 exceeding two, the proportional remaining-cap calculation and the 2005 wealth-value method for listed securities. Apply that current non-resident method using actual earlier disposals and asset history; the amount is not a fresh annual cap or a limit on gain. | Current LF128, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF130 | ## Deadlines and filing | Current LF130, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF132 | Residents include the gain/loss and exemption/election evidence in **Modelo 100 for the accrual year**, using [LIRPF art. 14](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a14) timing and the approved campaign window. Do not invent permanent 1 April/30 June dates for the 2026 return filed in 2027. Maintain lot records, cost/depreciation evidence, deferred/carryforward ledgers and later reinvestment proof. | Current LF132, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF134 | If reinvestment conditions fail, [RIRPF arts. 41/42](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a41) require the specified original-year supplementary declaration with interest in the special interval ending with the filing period for the failure year. A later sale and a new loss release do not automatically amend every old return. | Current LF134, exact original unit |

Other non-resident disposals have their own reporting/withheld-income rules. | Current LF136, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF138 | ## Penalties and interest | Current LF138, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF140 | Distinguish an unprompted late tax-due filing under [LGT art. 27](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2003-23186#a27), an assessed under-declaration under [art. 191](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2003-23186#a191), an information failure and a special relief-recapture supplement. The ordinary voluntary surcharge is 1% plus 1% per complete month through twelve months; after twelve months it is 15% with the specified subsequent interest, subject to the article's conditions. Use [arts. 26/28](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2003-23186#a26) for the actual interest/collection stage; do not impose a universal 150% gain penalty or charge a surcharge to an original-year reinvestment supplement without checking its special rule. | Current LF140, exact original unit |

| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF148 | ## Two worked examples that recompute | Current LF148, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF150 | ### 1. Ordinary disposal with costs | Current LF150, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF152 | A hypothetical common-territory resident sells an asset in 2026 for **20,000**, pays eligible selling costs 100, and has acquisition price 10,000 plus eligible acquisition costs 50. No exemption, depreciation, earlier losses or other savings income applies; their minimum is already fully allocated to general income. Transfer value 19,900 less acquisition value 10,050 gives gain **9,850**. Savings tax is `6,000 ×19% +3,850 ×21% =1,948.50` ([LIRPF arts. 35/66/76](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a35)). | Current LF152, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF154 | ```oa-check<br>name: Spain 2026 resident sale with acquisition and transfer costs<br>inputs:<br>  sale_price: 20000<br>  selling_costs: 100<br>  purchase_price: 10000<br>  purchase_costs: 50<br>steps:<br>  transfer_value: sale_price - selling_costs<br>  acquisition_value: purchase_price + purchase_costs<br>  gain: transfer_value - acquisition_value<br>  tax: min(gain, 6000) * 0.19 + max(gain - 6000, 0) * 0.21<br>expected:<br>  transfer_value: 19900<br>  acquisition_value: 10050<br>  gain: 9850<br>  tax: 1948.50<br>``` | Current LF154, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF173 | ### 2. Repurchase deferral and capped loss offset are different ledgers | Current LF173, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF175 | A hypothetical resident has realised transfer losses **10,000**, of which **2,000** is correctly allocated to homogeneous repurchased shares and deferred under art. 33.5. Thus current admitted transfer loss is 8,000. Positive investment income is 10,000, with no other items or prior losses and no remaining personal minimum. Cross-offset is at most 25% ×10,000 =**2,500**. Savings base is **7,500**, tax **1,455**. Carry **5,500 admitted loss** to the four following years and separately retain **2,000 deferred repurchase loss** until its release event ([LIRPF arts. 33.5/49](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a49)). | Current LF175, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF177 | ```oa-check<br>name: Spain 2026 repurchase-blocked loss and savings-pool cross-offset<br>inputs:<br>  realised_transfer_loss: 10000<br>  deferred_repurchase_loss: 2000<br>  positive_investment_income: 10000<br>steps:<br>  admitted_loss: realised_transfer_loss - deferred_repurchase_loss<br>  cross_offset: min(admitted_loss, positive_investment_income * 0.25)<br>  admitted_loss_carryforward: admitted_loss - cross_offset<br>  savings_base: positive_investment_income - cross_offset<br>  savings_tax: min(savings_base, 6000) * 0.19 + max(savings_base - 6000, 0) * 0.21<br>expected:<br>  admitted_loss: 8000<br>  cross_offset: 2500<br>  admitted_loss_carryforward: 5500<br>  savings_base: 7500<br>  savings_tax: 1455<br>``` | Current LF177, exact original unit |
